# Agents · B · part 16

1,026 entries, build-and-deploy to builder. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **build-and-deploy**: Scoped notes, moved from the root `AGENTS.md`: what is non-obvious and still true here. Paths are repo-relative. Project-wide rules stay in the root `AGENTS.md`. ([anolilab/neore](https://github.com/anolilab/neore/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/build-and-deploy.md) · ★14)
- **build-and-dev**: - Use a concrete simulator destination for `build-for-testing` and `test`. A *generic* destination (`generic/platform=iOS Simulator`) compiles arm64 and x86_64; through 1.0.7 that failed on the prebuilt RevenueCat module (`built for incompatible target`). RevenueCat was removed in 1.0.8 and the generic destination has not been re-tested since, so keep the concrete destination. - Simulator runtime ([mneves75/ai-pedometer](https://github.com/mneves75/ai-pedometer/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/build-and-dev.md))
- **build-and-dev-conventions**: &gt; TL;DR: The Kiali build is driven by a decomposed Makefile (root + nine `.mk` files), builds a CGO-disabled Go binary with version ldflags, embeds a pre-built React frontend, and pushes images to OpenShift, Minikube, or KinD registries through a unified `cluster-push` target. The operator is a filesystem symlink to a sibling repo. ([kiali/kiali](https://github.com/kiali/kiali/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/build-and-dev-conventions.md) · ★3.6k)
- **build-and-env**: - **`NEXT_PUBLIC_*` уезжает в бандл браузера.** Это не переменная окружения, а публичная константа, вшитая в сборку. Ключ, токен и строка подключения с таким префиксом считаются утёкшими. Секрет читается только в серверном коде. - **Значения `NEXT_PUBLIC_*` подставляются на сборке, а не на старте.** Смена значения без пересборки ничего не меняет; в образе Docker это значит build-arg, а не переменн ([KirillSachkov/education-platform](https://github.com/KirillSachkov/education-platform/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/build-and-env.md) · ★9)
- **build-and-review**: Every ticket goes through the same skills, in the same order, invoked by name with the Skill tool. This page is the rule, and it binds two readers: ([chizhangucb/tomte](https://github.com/chizhangucb/tomte/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/build-and-review.md))

- **build-and-test**: Everything runs through cargo-make; Rust tests use cargo-nextest. Both are pinned in `mise.toml`. `cargo make --list-all-steps` is the full menu. ([tasker-systems/temper](https://github.com/tasker-systems/temper/blob/HEAD/internal/agents/build-and-test.md) · ★6)
- **build-and-test**: This repo must be a sibling of a `fromsoftware-rs` checkout (the root crate uses `../fromsoftware-rs` path dependencies). ([Banon-Labs/er-mods-rs](https://github.com/Banon-Labs/er-mods-rs/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/build-and-test.md) · ★2)
- **build-and-test**: - Install: `uv sync` - Lint: `uv run ruff check .` - Types: `uv run mypy src` (strict) - Tests: `uv run pytest`; tests marked `slow` need a local SSH server and are excluded by default (`addopts` in `pyproject.toml`): `uv run pytest -m slow` to run them. - Build: `uv build` ([jrechet/harness-ai](https://github.com/jrechet/harness-ai/blob/HEAD/fixtures/repos/python-cli/repo/docs/agents/build-and-test.md) · ★1)
- **build-and-test**: 环境变量通过 `.env.example` 说明，运行时由 `src/config/mod.rs` 读取： ([ijkzen/llm-gateway](https://github.com/ijkzen/llm-gateway/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/build-and-test.md))
- **build-and-test**: **优先使用 Makefile 目标，不要直接调用 go 命令。** 运行 `make help` 查看所有可用目标。 ([L-ios/MystiSql](https://github.com/L-ios/MystiSql/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/build-and-test.md))
- **build-and-test**: Перед коммитом обязательно: `./gradlew check assembleDebug`. Агенту — через `scripts/agent/check.sh` (или флоу `agent.sh ship`): то же самое, но печатает только ошибки, упавшие тесты и итог (`Tests: N`, `OK`/`FAILED`) — экономит токены. ([winerar/co-shell](https://github.com/winerar/co-shell/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/build-and-test.md))
- **build-and-test-guide**: 1. Run `git status --short --branch` and inspect diffs. Preserve existing user modifications. 2. Verify Java with `java -version`. Existing GitHub workflows use Temurin JDK 21; Java 8 is insufficient for the current toolchain. 3. Use the checked-in Gradle wrapper. Do not upgrade Gradle, Kotlin, AGP, or dependencies just to work around a source error. 4. Discover tasks first: Windows `.\gradlew.bat ([habibbrox2/DoraiAi](https://github.com/habibbrox2/DoraiAi/blob/HEAD/agents/build-and-test-guide.md))
